# 雷云 4 前端功能全清单（自动生成）

> **来源**：雷云前端语言包 `trans-zh-CN` / `trans-en`，在 Node 里执行其 webpack
> 模块后导出（`.ref/tools/extract-locale.js`）。
> **共 4992 条**，分 1101 个命名空间。
>
> 本清单里的每一条都是雷云**自己的界面文案**，不是推测。
> 重新生成：`node .ref/tools/gen-feature-md.js`

| `TEXT_SOFTWARE_CHROMA_APP_DESC` | 借助约 1,680 万种色彩选项和多种独特的灯光效果，我们极具代表性的 RGB 灯光系统成功入驻全球游戏玩家的家中。 我们提供了独一元二的 RGB 灯光定制功能，让你可以轻松通过集成了 Chroma 幻彩功能的游戏来增强自己的沉浸式体验，或为自己打造理想的室内游戏环境。 | With 16.8 million colors and a suite of unique lighting effects, our iconic RGB lighting system has made its way onto the homes of gamers worldwide. Whether it’s enhancing your immersion through Chroma-integrated games, or designing your dream gaming room setup, we offer a level of RGB lighting customization that can’t be found anywhere else. |
| `TEXT_SOFTWARE_CORTEX_DESC` | 借助 Razer Cortex 雷游解锁电脑的全部潜能，提升游戏体验。 无论是在网络上寻找最给力的游戏优惠，还是在游戏或直播游戏精彩时刻时提高电脑的性能，Cortex 雷游都是理想的免费多合一软件，可让你获得理想的游戏体验。 | Unlock your PC’s full potential and enhance your gameplay with Razer Cortex. From combing the web for the best game deals, improving your PC performance while gaming or broadcasting your in-game moments, Cortex is the best free all-in-one software to get the best out of your gaming sessions. |
| `TEXT_SOFTWARE_PROMOTION` | Software Promotions | Software Promotions |
| `TEXT_SOFTWARE_SURROUND_SOUND` | 你购买的 Razer 雷蛇耳麦配备 7.1 环绕声软件，可提供精确的声音定位和逼真的游戏体验。 下载应用程序并使用你的激活码进行注册，以便在游戏中占据优势。 | The purchase of your Razer headset comes with 7.1 surround sound software for superior positional audio and a lifelike gaming experience. Download the application and register with your activation code to gain a competitive gaming advantage. |

## `ZERO`（8 条）

| key | 中文 | English |
|---|---|---|
| `ZERO_DEADZONES_WARNING` | 零死区 | Zero Deadzones |
| `ZERO_DEADZONES_WARNING_DESC_1` | 将死区设定为 0% 将录入所有移动，让你能像职业玩家般操作，但也会录入意外的输入（可能表现为“摇杆漂移”现象）。 | Setting a 0% deadzone will register every movement, so you can play like a pro, but will also register unintended inputs (which can appear like "stick drift"). |
| `ZERO_DEADZONES_WARNING_DESC_2` | 将死区设定为 0% 会使摇杆的细微移动都能被识别，这将带来极其灵敏的操控体验，但也增加了意外输入的概率。 | Setting the deadzone to 0% makes every tiny stick movement register, resulting in very sensitive controls but also a higher chance of unintended inputs. |
| `ZERO_DEADZONE_HIGHLIGHT_DESC` | 将死区设定为 0% 会大幅提升灵敏度，但会导致出现意外的微小输入。 | Deadzones set to 0% dramatically increases sensitivity and will result in unintended micro-inputs. |
| `ZERO_DEADZONE_HIGHLIGHT_TIP` | 将死区设定为 0% 会大幅提升灵敏度，导致即使未触碰拇指控制杆时也会出现类似“摇杆漂移”的意外微小输入。偏好高灵敏度的职业玩家已习惯这种操作方式，但不建议休闲玩家采用。 | Setting the deadzone to 0% greatly increases sensitivity, resulting in unintended micro-inputs that resemble "stick drift" even when the thumbsticks aren’t touched. Pro players who prefer high sensitivity are used to this behavior, but it's not recommended for casual gamers. |

## `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP`（1 条）

| key | 中文 | English |
|---|---|---|
| `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP` | 通过“智能追踪”功能，不管是哪种表面，用户都可以设置首选追踪距离。 | "Smart Tracking" allows the user to set a preferred tracking distance regardless of the surface. |

## `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP1`（1 条）

| key | 中文 | English |
|---|---|---|
| `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP1` | 微调鼠标的抬升和着陆幅度，让鼠标无论置于哪种表面，都能提供更优秀的精准度和准确性。 | Fine-tune the mouse's lift-off and landing levels for improved precision and accuracy, irrespective of the surface it's on. |

## `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP2`（1 条）

| key | 中文 | English |
|---|---|---|
| `SMARTTRACKINGTOOLTIP2` | “低”追踪距离意味着鼠标稍微离开表面就会停止追踪移动。与此相反，“高”追踪距离允许鼠标在被抬离表面相当高的位置时仍能继续追踪移动。<br><br>建议将追踪距离设置为“低”，以避免在抬起鼠标时意外移动光标。 | A "Low" tracking distance means that the mouse stops tracking movement when it is slightly lifted off the surface. In contrast, a "High" tracking distance allows the mouse to continue tracking movement even when it is lifted considerably above the surface.<br><br>It is recommended to set the tracking distance to "Low" to avoid accidentally moving the cursor when lifting the mouse. |
